    index3 = p1
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('primals_10', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_1 = ops.load('buf9', get_index_1)
        constant = ops.constant(-3.4028234663852886e+38, torch.float32)
        where = ops.where(load, load_1, constant)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index2')
        load_2 = ops.load('buf0', get_index_2)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index3')
        load_3 = ops.load('buf0', get_index_3)
        sub = ops.sub(load_2, load_3)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(1, torch.int64)
        ge = ops.ge(sub, constant_1)
        constant_2 = ops.constant(-3.4028234663852886e+38, torch.float32)
        constant_3 = ops.constant(0.0, torch.float32)
        where_1 = ops.where(ge, constant_2, constant_3)

        load_4 = ops.load('buf0', get_index_4)
        get_index_5 = self.get_index('index3')

        gt = ops.gt(load_4, load_5)

        to_dtype_1 = ops.to_dtype(load_6, torch.float32, src_dtype = torch.int64)

        eq = ops.eq(add, constant_4)

        load_7 = ops.load('buf0', get_index_7)
        get_index_8 = self.get_index('index3')
        load_8 = ops.load('buf0', get_index_8)
        sub_1 = ops.sub(load_7, load_8)
        constant_5 = ops.constant(1, torch.int64)
        ge_1 = ops.ge(sub_1, constant_5)
        constant_6 = ops.constant(-3.4028234663852886e+38, torch.float32)
        constant_7 = ops.constant(0.0, torch.float32)
